│TEXT                        │WINDOW Main TEXT "testo"                                          │Writes text into the given window.        │
│THEME                       │WINDOW THEME "dark"                                               │Sets the global window theme.             │
│VIEWPORT                    │VIEWPORT [SCREEN] (x1, y1)-(x2, y2)                               │Sets the logical coordinate window.       │
│DESKTOP                     │WINDOW DESKTOP 2                                                  │Changes the active virtual desktop.       │
│HEIGHT                      │x = WINDOWS.HEIGHT()                                              │Window height -> LONG.                    │
│THEME                       │WINDOW THEME "dark"                                               │Sets the global window theme.             │
│WIDTH                       │x = WINDOWS.WIDTH()                                               │Window width -> LONG.                     │
│WINDOWCLOSED                │x = WINDOWS.WINDOWCLOSED()                                        │-> LONG                                   │
│X                           │x = WINDOWS.X()                                                   │Window X coordinate -> LONG.              │
│Y                           │x = WINDOWS.Y()                                                   │Window Y coordinate -> LONG.              │
